Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The rooms operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 115. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 655 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to rooms theory, we decompose the problem into 115 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 655. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
